# Dreamboard SDK Agent Reference

Generated from the published declaration facades in `packages/sdk/dist`.
Do not edit this file directly; run `pnpm docs:generate`.

## Mental Model

1. Author the manifest and let codegen produce a workspace contract.
2. Bind that contract once with `createContractAuthoring(contract)`.
3. Define game state, phases, interactions, and views through the bound authoring object.
4. Reducer code reads with `q`, mutates through `tx`, and returns `accept` or `reject`.
5. Runtime/UI code consumes generated workspace-contract values and SDK primitives.
6. If artifacts go stale, regenerate with `dreamboard test generate` before debugging authored code.

## Decision Table

| I want to...                                       | Use...                                            |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| bind generated contract facts to authoring helpers | `createContractAuthoring(contract)`               |
| define top-level game state and errors             | bound `game(...)`                                 |
| define a phase                                     | bound `phase(name)(...)`                          |
| define a view projection                           | bound `view(...)`                                 |
| validate an action                                 | an interaction `rule`                             |
| explain why an interaction is blocked              | `ctx.explain` or testing availability matchers    |
| read table state in reducer code                   | `q` table queries                                 |
| change table state in reducer code                 | `tx` transaction operations                       |
| accept an interaction                              | `accept(...)`                                     |
| reject an interaction                              | `reject(...)`                                     |
| store turn-scoped state                            | phase state schema                                |
| reference generated ids                            | generated contract `ids` and `literals`           |
| render plugin UI                                   | `PluginRuntime` and runtime primitives            |
| inspect exact installed API offline                | `node_modules/@dreamboard-games/sdk/REFERENCE.md` |

### defineStaticView

```ts
function defineStaticView<Contract extends AnyReducerGameContract>(): <
  Projection,
>(
  definition: StaticViewDefinition<
    ExactManifestContractOf<Contract>,
    Projection
  >,
) => StaticViewDefinition<ExactManifestContractOf<Contract>, Projection>;
```

Factory for the session-scoped static view (see {@link StaticViewDefinition}). Kept separate from {@link defineView} because the argument shape is structurally different: it exposes only the manifest and generated static queries, with no `state`, `playerId`, `runtime`, `fx`, `ops`, or `accept/reject`. That shape is what prevents authors from accidentally projecting per-tick state into the once-per-session payload.

### perPlayerEntries

```ts
function perPlayerEntries<Value, Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<Value, Id>,
): ReadonlyArray<readonly [Id, Value]>;
```

Ordered `[id, value]` pairs. Returns the backing array as-is; callers must not mutate it.

### perPlayerGet

```ts
function perPlayerGet<Value, Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<Value, Id>,
  id: Id,
): Value | undefined;
```

Lookup with no fallback; returns `undefined` for missing seats.

### perPlayerHas

```ts
function perPlayerHas<Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<unknown, Id>,
  id: Id,
): boolean;
```

Whether `id` has a value.

### perPlayerKeys

```ts
function perPlayerKeys<Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<unknown, Id>,
): Id[];
```

Ordered list of seat ids present in the `PerPlayer`.

### perPlayerMap

```ts
function perPlayerMap<Value, Next, Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<Value, Id>,
  f: (v: Value, id: Id, index: number) => Next,
): PerPlayer<Next, Id>;
```

Return a new `PerPlayer` where each value is replaced by `f(value, id)`. Seat order is preserved.

### perPlayerRequire

```ts
function perPlayerRequire<Value, Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<Value, Id>,
  id: Id,
): Value;
```

Lookup that throws if `id` is not present.

### perPlayerSchema

```ts
  valueSchema: z.ZodType<Value>,
  options?: PerPlayerSchemaOptions<Id>,
): z.ZodType<PerPlayer<Value, Id>>;
```

```ts
  readonly playerIdSchema?: z.ZodType<Id>;
  readonly players?: readonly Id[];
};
```

Options accepted by `perPlayerSchema`.

### perPlayerSet

```ts
function perPlayerSet<Value, Id extends PlayerId>(
  value: PerPlayer<Value, Id>,
  id: Id,
  next: Value,
): PerPlayer<Value, Id>;
```

Return a new `PerPlayer` with `id`'s value replaced (or added if the id is not present). Preserves entry order; new entries are appended.

### perPlayerSize

```ts
function perPlayerSize(value: PerPlayer<unknown, PlayerId>): number;
```

Number of seats in the `PerPlayer`.

### perPlayerValues

```ts
function perPlayerValues<Value>(value: PerPlayer<Value, PlayerId>): Value[];
```

Ordered list of values present in the `PerPlayer`.

### PhaseMapOf

```ts
type PhaseMapOf = ...;
```

Heterogeneous phase map for a contract. Each phase carries its own local `PhaseStateSchema` plus authoring-time Actions/Flows/Interactions/ Stages/Zones maps. The registries are bound to the contract's base state + manifest so the runtime can iterate them through a single index type without reaching for `any`.

Base state shape every collector is generic over. Collectors that need narrowed ids (card / player) use `PlayerIdOfState<State>` etc. to thread the manifest's branded types.

### EffectRollDieDefinition

```ts
type EffectRollDieDefinition = ...;
```

`rollDie` effect. Resolves a `rollDie` wire effect. `reduce` / `context` are both optional so authors can fire-and-forget a die roll without observing the result.

### EffectShuffleDefinition

```ts
type EffectShuffleDefinition = ...;
```

`shuffleSharedZone` effect. Resolves a `shuffleSharedZone` wire effect. `reduce` / `context` are both optional.

### EffectShufflePlayerZoneDefinition

```ts
type EffectShufflePlayerZoneDefinition = ...;
```

`shufflePlayerZone` effect. Resolves a `shufflePlayerZone` wire effect for a single player's perPlayer zone (e.g. deck-builder reshuffle of discard into deck). `reduce` / `context` are both optional.

### EffectSpecLike

```ts
type EffectSpecLike = ...;
```

Structural shape of the objects produced by `defineEffect`, as seen by the `fx.effect` dispatcher. The public, per-type effect definitions live in `model/spec.ts`.

### EffectTypeTag

```ts
type EffectTypeTag = "rollDie" | "shuffleSharedZone" | "shufflePlayerZone";
```

Marker tag attached by `defineEffect` to every effect spec. `fx.effect` uses `type` to dispatch to the right wire-effect builder.

### PhaseMapOf

```ts
type PhaseMapOf = ...;
```

Heterogeneous phase map for a contract. Each phase carries its own local `PhaseStateSchema` plus authoring-time Actions/Flows/Interactions/ Stages/Zones maps. The registries are bound to the contract's base state + manifest so the runtime can iterate them through a single index type without reaching for `any`.

### PhaseStateMapOfDefinitions

```ts
type PhaseStateMapOfDefinitions = ...;
```

Helpers below accept heterogeneous phase maps (each phase binds its own `PhaseStateSchema` + per-phase registries). TypeScript requires the constraint to use a shape compatible with contract-bound state types in a contravariant `initialState` position, so we erase the registry-bound state/manifest generics with `any`. The only purpose of the constraint is to guarantee `Definitions[Name]` is a `PhaseDefinition` so the `extends` check below can infer `PhaseStateSchema`.

### StaticViewDefinition

```ts
type StaticViewDefinition<
  Manifest extends ManifestContract<RuntimeTableRecord>,
  Projection = unknown,
> = {
  project: (args: {
    manifest: Manifest;
    q: StaticViewQueries<Manifest>;
  }) => Projection;
};
```

Session-scoped, once-per-init view. The `project` callback receives only the authored manifest — the mutable-state helpers (`state`, `playerId`, `runtime`, `fx`, `ops`, `accept`, `reject`, `q`) that `ViewDefinition.project` exposes are structurally absent, so an author cannot accidentally project per-tick state into the payload. The host calls this once per reducer session, caches the result, and merges it back into every seat view on the client. Moving static board topology here is what lets the adapter skip the ~87% of `projectSeatsDynamic` wall time that used to re-serialize manifest-sourced fields on every input.

### PlayerColor

```ts
interface PlayerColor {
  readonly solid: string;
  readonly soft: string;
  readonly on: string;
}
```

Per-player color tokens. The {@link Theme.player} array is indexed by 0-based seat slot (`player[0]` = first seat). Each entry carries a `solid` brand color, a `soft` tint usable as a background, and `on` — the recommended foreground when text sits on `solid`.

### Theme

```ts
interface Theme { ... }
```

The complete theme contract. Every leaf is a CSS-ready string so the provider can emit a CSS variable for each path without per-leaf formatting. {@link DeepPartial} is exported for author overrides.

### ThemeContextValue

```ts
interface ThemeContextValue {
  /** Fully resolved theme. */
  readonly theme: Theme;
  /** CSS-var map consumable as `style` on a wrapper. */
  readonly cssVars: CSSProperties;
}
```

Subset returned by {@link useTheme} — the resolved theme plus a stable copy of its CSS-variable map so consumers can reuse it (for example, to apply tokens to a portal that escapes the provider).

### useIsMobile


Hook to detect if the user is on a mobile/small screen device

### useMobileHandTrayActive

```ts
function useMobileHandTrayActive(): boolean;
```

Whether the mobile hand tray is currently presenting hands — i.e. the viewport is below the mobile breakpoint and at least one primary/auxiliary hand has registered. Authors can use this to drop redundant inline hand chrome (labels, framing) that the tray already provides, instead of guessing the breakpoint with a CSS media query. Returns `false` outside `<UI.Root>`.

### useMobileHandTrayActive

```ts
function useMobileHandTrayActive(): boolean;
```

Whether the mobile hand tray is currently presenting hands — i.e. the viewport is below the mobile breakpoint and at least one primary/auxiliary hand has registered. Authors can use this to drop redundant inline hand chrome (labels, framing) that the tray already provides, instead of guessing the breakpoint with a CSS media query. Returns `false` outside `<UI.Root>`.

## @dreamboard-games/sdk/runtime/primitives

### AuthoredCardIntent

```ts
type AuthoredCardIntent =
  | {
      type: "activate";
      cardId: string;
      source: "tap" | "keyboard";
    }
  | {
      type: "previewStart";
      cardId: string;
    }
  | {
      type: "previewEnd";
      cardId: string;
    }
  | {
      type: "drop";
      cardId: string;
      targetId: string;
      source: "pointer" | "keyboard";
    };
```

Author-facing card intent. Mirrors the SDK shape, but for `drop` intents `targetId` is the manifest id (e.g. `"hex-a"`) — the runtime adapter decodes the opaque SDK target id before invoking author callbacks. This matches the type the generated `hand.Hand` facade declares.

### decodeAuthoredCardIntent

```ts
function decodeAuthoredCardIntent(intent: CardIntent): AuthoredCardIntent;
```

Translate a raw SDK `CardIntent` into the author-facing `AuthoredCardIntent` shape. For drop intents the opaque encoded `targetId` is decoded into its manifest value; non-decodable target ids fall through unchanged so authors can detect and ignore them.

### descriptorsHaveDestinationInput

```ts
function descriptorsHaveDestinationInput(state: PluginStateSnapshot): boolean;
```

Determine whether any descriptor in the available list expects a board or card destination input alongside a card target. Used by generated facades to decide whether to mount a drag surface even before the author wires dropTargets.

### dropTargetIdFor

```ts
function dropTargetIdFor(kind: RuntimeDropTargetKind, value: string): string;
```

Convenience helper for builders of generated hand surfaces. Encodes a typed target kind plus value into the opaque `targetId` that the SDK presents to its drop pipeline. The runtime adapter resolves the kind to the matching descriptor input at drop time, so authors never need to know collector input names. Generated typed targets call this internally.

### useResolvedCardTargetValue

```ts
function useResolvedCardTargetValue(): unknown;
```

Live draft value for the active interaction's card-target input, resolved from the surrounding `<Interaction.Root>`. Returns the selected card-id array for `selection: "many"` inputs and the single id for `selection: "one"`. Returns `undefined` when there is no interaction context or no card-target input (so it is safe to render outside a root). Reactive: `handle.values` updates as the draft changes. Backs the card surface `slot.card.Value`.

## @dreamboard-games/sdk/runtime/runtime-api

### PluginSessionState

```ts
interface PluginSessionState { ... }
```

Plugin session state

### PluginStateSnapshot

```ts
interface PluginStateSnapshot { ... }
```

The complete state snapshot sent to the plugin via state-sync. This is the single source of truth for all reducer-native plugin state.

### RuntimeAPI

```ts
interface RuntimeAPI { ... }
```

RuntimeAPI provides the interface between plugin code and the game runtime. This API is exposed to plugin iframes for subscribing to game events and submitting actions.

### SubmissionError

```ts
interface SubmissionError extends Error {
  /** Machine-readable error code when available */
  errorCode?: string;
}
```

Structured authoritative submission failure returned by runtime submit APIs.

### ValidationResult

```ts
interface ValidationResult {
  /** Whether the action is valid */
  valid: boolean;
  /** Machine-readable error code if validation failed */
  errorCode?: string;
  /** Human-readable error message if validation failed */
  message?: string;
}
```

Result of validating a player action

### ZoneHandlesSnapshot

```ts
interface ZoneHandlesSnapshot<InteractionType extends string = string> {
  cardIds: readonly string[];
  cardViewsById: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  playableByCardId: Readonly<
    Record<string, ReadonlyArray<InteractionDescriptor<InteractionType>>>
  >;
}
```

Per-player view of a single zone. Mirrors the ZoneHandles wire shape from the trusted bundle's resolveZoneHandles; `cardViewsById` is JSON-serialized `ViewCard` and `playableByCardId` lists the interactions that are playable on each card (eligibility already filtered by each interaction's validate).
